Beauty for long-term investors due to its expanding customer base and international growth potential, despite Wendy's turnaround efforts.
<<<INSIGHTS>>>
ELF | positive | Strong 39.9% revenue growth, 31 consecutive quarters of sales increases, expanding international presence, and successful brand portfolio (rhode, Naturium). Latest quarter showed 51% YoY sales growth with raised guidance. Analyst recommends as better buy for 2027 despite higher valuation and debt concerns.
WEN | negative | Declining U.S. sales, pulled full-year outlook, cut dividend, and flat revenue performance (-0.3% YoY). While offering stability and 5.52% dividend yield, the company faces customer traffic challenges and significant debt (35.3x debt-to-equity ratio). Analyst views it as weaker choice compared to e.l.f. Beauty.
